# Commands · A · part 5

1,423 entries, aeo-baseline-instructions to agentrelay:status. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **aeo-baseline-instructions**: Guided setup for Build 4 (the baseline), from nothing. Holds the learner's hand through setting up Google Analytics 4 and Search Console if they don't have them, connecting both, and producing a day-zero report of what AI traffic is worth, from first-party data only. One step at a time, with what to do and what to expect. ([conversionxl/cxl-personal-os-plugins](https://github.com/conversionxl/cxl-personal-os-plugins/blob/HEAD/commands/aeo-baseline-instructions.md) · ★2)
- **aeo-canonical-instructions**: Guided setup for the canonical description. Holds the learner's hand from "every page says something different" to one approved way of saying what the company is, rolled out across the site, social profiles and third-party listings, with an audit, a swap sheet, a live check and a re-check command. ([conversionxl/cxl-personal-os-plugins](https://github.com/conversionxl/cxl-personal-os-plugins/blob/HEAD/commands/aeo-canonical-instructions.md) · ★2)
